# Graph Report - investment-agent-main  (2026-09-20)

## Corpus Check
- 1321 files · ~1,432,669 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 17099 nodes · 36919 edges · 792 communities (631 shown, 124 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2085 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `12f646b2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
